classical actions containing the natural special group, and let $K$ be the full ordered splitting

image--kernel data into the dual annihilator data. For sufficiently large $\dim W$, the subgroup $K$
is maximal in $M$.

same thermal parameters and diameter at most $2S+20$;
conditional on the complete revelation record, its
interface has the ordinary Dobrushin FK law;
every deterministic sequence of possible successful
outputs $(\widehat\Omega_n,\widehat a_n,\widehat b_n)$
converges in the marked Carath\'eodory sense to
$(\Omega,a,b)$;
the normalized coordinates on the retained domain
are uniformly $o(1)$ from its original normalized
coordinates, including prime-end occurrences, and
the removed endpoint heads have vanishing disc
diameter.

Its input is an explicitly listed simple graph $G=(V,E)$, with

sizes in $(1/6,1)$ such that:
if $G$ has a vertex cover of size at most $k$, the items pack into
$B$ unit-capacity bins;
any packing into at most $B+c$ bins yields a vertex cover of size
at most $k+\rho n$.
